
CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS uhc_database;
USE uhc_database;

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS country_uhc;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS region_uhc;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS country_info;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS region_info;

CREATE TABLE region_info (
    region_code VARCHAR(10) PRIMARY KEY,
    region_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE
);

CREATE TABLE country_info (
    iso3c CHAR(3) PRIMARY KEY,
    country_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE
);

CREATE TABLE country_uhc (
    iso3c CHAR(3) NOT NULL,
    year INT NOT NULL,
    fbp1_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    fbp1_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    fbp2_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    fbp2_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    fbpr_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    fbpr_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    nop1_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    nop1_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    nop2_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    nop2_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    nopr_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    nopr_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    oopc10_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    oopc10_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    oopc25_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    oopc25_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    PRIMARY KEY (iso3c, year),
    FOREIGN KEY (iso3c) REFERENCES country_info(iso3c)
);

CREATE TABLE region_uhc (
    region_code CHAR(3) NOT NULL,
    year INT NOT NULL,
    fbp1_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    fbp1_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    fbp2_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    fbp2_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    fbpr_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    fbpr_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    nop1_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    nop1_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    nop2_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    nop2_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    nopr_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    nopr_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    oopc10_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    oopc10_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    oopc25_total DECIMAL(12,2),
    oopc25_percent DECIMAL(12,2),
    PRIMARY KEY (region_code, year),
    FOREIGN KEY (region_code) REFERENCES region_info(region_code)
);

-- ============================================================
-- 5. CRUD QUERIES
-- ============================================================

-- ADD A COUNTRY
-- INSERT INTO country_info (iso3c, country_name)
-- VALUES ('IND', 'India');

-- UPDATE A COUNTRY
-- UPDATE country_info
-- SET country_name = 'India'
-- WHERE iso3c = 'IND';

-- DELETE A COUNTRY
-- DELETE FROM country_info
-- WHERE iso3c = 'IND';

-- ADD A REGION
-- INSERT INTO region_info (region_code, region_name)
-- VALUES ('TEST', 'Test Region');

-- UPDATE A REGION
-- UPDATE region_info
-- SET region_name = 'Updated Region'
-- WHERE region_code = 'TEST';

-- DELETE A REGION
-- DELETE FROM region_info
-- WHERE region_code = 'TEST';

-- INSERT A NEW YEARLY COUNTRY RECORD
-- INSERT INTO country_uhc (iso3c, year, fbp1_percent, fbp2_percent)
-- VALUES ('IND', 2024, 10.50, 20.75);

-- UPDATE A YEARLY COUNTRY RECORD
-- UPDATE country_uhc
-- SET fbp1_percent = 12.00
-- WHERE iso3c = 'IND' AND year = 2024;

-- DELETE A YEARLY COUNTRY RECORD
-- DELETE FROM country_uhc
-- WHERE iso3c = 'IND' AND year = 2024;


-- ============================================================
-- 6. SEARCH QUERIES
-- ============================================================

-- SEARCH BY COUNTRY
SELECT c.country_name, u.year, u.fbp1_percent, u.fbp2_percent
FROM country_info c
JOIN country_uhc u ON c.iso3c = u.iso3c
WHERE c.country_name = 'India'
ORDER BY u.year;

-- SEARCH BY REGION
SELECT r.region_name, u.year, u.fbp1_percent, u.fbp2_percent
FROM region_info r
JOIN region_uhc u ON r.region_code = u.region_code
WHERE r.region_name = 'South Asia'
ORDER BY u.year;

-- SEARCH BY YEAR
SELECT c.country_name, u.year, u.fbp1_percent, u.fbp2_percent
FROM country_info c
JOIN country_uhc u ON c.iso3c = u.iso3c
WHERE u.year = 2020
ORDER BY c.country_name;


-- ============================================================
-- 7. COUNTRY-WISE REPORTS
-- ============================================================

-- Country-wise average indicators
SELECT c.country_name,
       AVG(u.fbp1_percent) AS avg_fbp1,
       AVG(u.fbp2_percent) AS avg_fbp2,
       AVG(u.fbpr_percent) AS avg_fbpr
FROM country_info c
JOIN country_uhc u ON c.iso3c = u.iso3c
GROUP BY c.iso3c, c.country_name
ORDER BY c.country_name;

-- Country-wise report for one year
SELECT c.country_name, u.year,
       u.fbp1_percent, u.fbp2_percent,
       u.fbpr_percent, u.oopc10_percent, u.oopc25_percent
FROM country_info c
JOIN country_uhc u ON c.iso3c = u.iso3c
WHERE u.year = 2020
ORDER BY c.country_name;


-- ============================================================
-- 8. REGION-WISE REPORTS
-- ============================================================

-- Region-wise average indicators
SELECT r.region_name,
       AVG(u.fbp1_percent) AS avg_fbp1,
       AVG(u.fbp2_percent) AS avg_fbp2,
       AVG(u.fbpr_percent) AS avg_fbpr
FROM region_info r
JOIN region_uhc u ON r.region_code = u.region_code
GROUP BY r.region_code, r.region_name
ORDER BY r.region_name;

-- Region-wise report for one year
SELECT r.region_name, u.year,
       u.fbp1_percent, u.fbp2_percent,
       u.fbpr_percent, u.oopc10_percent, u.oopc25_percent
FROM region_info r
JOIN region_uhc u ON r.region_code = u.region_code
WHERE u.year = 2020
ORDER BY r.region_name;


-- ============================================================
-- 9. COMPARE INDICATORS ACROSS YEARS
-- ============================================================

SELECT year,
       AVG(fbp1_percent) AS avg_fbp1,
       AVG(fbp2_percent) AS avg_fbp2,
       AVG(fbpr_percent) AS avg_fbpr
FROM country_uhc
GROUP BY year
ORDER BY year;

-- Compare one country's indicator across years
SELECT c.country_name, u.year, u.fbp1_percent, u.fbp2_percent
FROM country_info c
JOIN country_uhc u ON c.iso3c = u.iso3c
WHERE c.country_name = 'India'
ORDER BY u.year;


-- ============================================================
-- 10. STATISTICAL SUMMARIES
-- ============================================================

SELECT
    COUNT(*) AS total_records,
    AVG(fbp1_percent) AS average_fbp1,
    MAX(fbp1_percent) AS maximum_fbp1,
    MIN(fbp1_percent) AS minimum_fbp1,
    AVG(fbp2_percent) AS average_fbp2,
    MAX(fbp2_percent) AS maximum_fbp2,
    MIN(fbp2_percent) AS minimum_fbp2
FROM country_uhc;

-- Year-wise statistical summary
SELECT
    year,
    COUNT(*) AS records,
    AVG(fbp1_percent) AS average_fbp1,
    MAX(fbp1_percent) AS maximum_fbp1,
    MIN(fbp1_percent) AS minimum_fbp1
FROM country_uhc
GROUP BY year
ORDER BY year;


-- ============================================================
-- 11. USEFUL ADDITIONAL QUERIES
-- ============================================================

-- Show all countries
SELECT * FROM country_info ORDER BY country_name;

-- Show all regions
SELECT * FROM region_info ORDER BY region_name;

-- Count records by country
SELECT c.country_name, COUNT(*) AS total_records
FROM country_info c
JOIN country_uhc u ON c.iso3c = u.iso3c
GROUP BY c.iso3c, c.country_name
ORDER BY total_records DESC;

-- Find records where FBP1 percentage is greater than 20
SELECT c.country_name, u.year, u.fbp1_percent
FROM country_info c
JOIN country_uhc u ON c.iso3c = u.iso3c
WHERE u.fbp1_percent > 20
ORDER BY u.fbp1_percent DESC;
